<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f75e34","wall_time_seconds":0.2017014,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1949,"output":"---\\r\\nname: bootcamp-builder\\r\\ndescription: Build a complete repo-native LSAT U bootcamp for a new topic, cloning the Intro to LR (Main Conclusion) build process end to end. Interviews Joshua, ingests the content files he feeds it plus a scan of /Curriculum/{Topic}/, then drives a gated multi-session build inside the LSATU repo (src/bootcamps/{slug}/) until bootcamp-parity-audit passes against Intro to LR. Replaces lsat-u-topic-builder. Use when Joshua says \\"build a [topic] bootcamp\\", \\"new bootcamp\\", \\"spin up [topic]\\", \\"/bootcamp-builder [topic]\\", or wants to replicate the Intro to LR process for another topic.\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Bootcamp Builder\\r\\n\\r\\nTurn a topic + content into a shipped, repo-native LSAT U bootcamp that matches Intro to LR quality. This is the productized version of the Intro to LR (Main Conclusion) build process. It replaces `lsat-u-topic-builder`.\\r\\n\\r\\n**The template is the code, not a doc:** `C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/bootcamps/main-conclusion/` (the Intro to LR build). Read it before scaffolding anything. Its structure:\\r\\n\\r\\n```\\r\\nsrc/bootcamps/{slug}/\\r\\n  App.tsx, routes.tsx        # nested <BootcampRoutes/> mounted under /bootcamp/{slug}/*\\r\\n  components/                # workspace-shell (chrome), drills, pickers, coach\'s note, command palette, trap-master, smart-hints...\\r\\n  content/*.source.ts        # authored content as typed TS sources + schemas.ts\\r\\n  data/*.generated.json      # generated from sources via scripts/{slug}/import-content.ts\\r\\n  modules/                   # lessons, reference, drills, practice-drills, simulator, hard-sentences, diagnostics, journal, settings, ModuleIndex\\r\\n  persistence/               # Persistence interface, LocalStorage/IndexedDB/SupabaseSynced, slug-prefixed keys\\r\\n  hooks/ lib/ styles/ types/\\r\\n  __tests__/impeccable-bans.test.ts\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Hard rules (never violate)\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Design: reuse Intro to LR exactly.** Same components, tokens, chrome, look. No per-topic themes, no bronze accents by default. A student moving between bootcamps sees one product.\\r\\n- **Pedagogy locked:** teach-first calibrate-after, never cold-test; warm-coach voice; preserve Joshua\'s exact phrasings verbatim; no em dashes anywhere.\\r\\n- **Quality bar:** `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md` in the vault. ModuleIntro is a real lesson; mastery tracking not attempt counting; reveal panels teach; no dead clicks, no fake analytics, no raw localStorage as UI text.\\r\\n- **Structure:** clone the Intro to LR module set by default. Deviations (drop/add/merge modules for topic fit) are PROPOSED at Gate 2 and require Joshua\'s approval.\\r\\n- **No invented LSAT content silently.** Content comes from Joshua\'s files. Gaps are flagged; Joshua decides (he authors, Claude drafts for his review, or defer to v1.5).\\r\\n- **Git:** Lovable pushes to the same origin/main. Always `git fetch` first, commit path-scoped (`git commit -- <paths>`, check `git status` for pre-staged files), never force-push. After clean diff + green build, commit and push without asking.\\r\\n- **Missing reviewed content counts as build failure**, not something to paper over.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Multi-session continuity (vault checkpointing)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe build spans many sessions. State lives in the Obsidian vault (`~/ai-os`):\\r\\n\\r\\n1. On Gate 0, create `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/synthesis/{slug}-bootcamp-build.md` with a **Gate ledger** (Gate 0..5, each `pending / in progress / APPROVED YYYY-MM-DD`), module checklist, decisions, and open questions.\\r\\n2. Add a `## Now` item to `wiki/tasks.md`: `{Topic} bootcamp build (gate N)`.\\r\\n3. After EVERY gate approval and at every session end, update the build page + append one line to `wiki/log.md`.\\r\\n4. A fresh session resumes by reading the build page first, then jumping to the current gate. Never redo an APPROVED gate.\\r\\n\\r\\n## The gated process\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 0: Interview (no files touched yet)\\r\\n\\r\\nGrill Joshua (AskUserQuestion, batched, opinionated options). Cover:\\r\\n- Topic name, slug, route target (`/bootcamp/{slug}`)\\r\\n- What content files he will feed it, and where they are\\r\\n- Topic\'s named tools, must-preserve worked examples, signature traps (his versions of FABS / Trojan Horse)\\r\\n- Which Intro to LR modules he suspects won\'t fit this topic\\r\\n- Priority order and anything explicitly out of scope\\r\\n\\r\\nCreate the vault build page, record answers. → **Joshua approves scope.**\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 1: Content intake + audit\\r\\n\\r\\nSources = BOTH: (a) every file Joshua fed it, and (b) a scan of `C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/Claude/02_PROJECTS/lsat-u/Curriculum/{Topic}/` (Notes/, Homework/, megadocs, scans) — flag anything relevant he didn\'t mention, plus a light `mdfind` sweep for topic files elsewhere on the Mac.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor every file: read it fully; summarize unique teaching content; note overlap; extract named tools, worked examples, voicy passages (mark verbatim-preserve). Tier-grade S/A/B/C/D:\\r\\n- **S** foundation, preserve verbatim where possible; **A** use heavily; **B** integrate selectively; **C** mine one block, drop rest; **D** unique-content-free, recommend drop.\\r\\n\\r\\nMap the corpus against every Intro to LR module (lessons, reference vault, indicator vault, flashcards, drills, practice drills, simulator + trap master, hard sentences, diagnostics, journal): `sufficient / marginal / insufficient` each. For each gap, ask Joshua: he authors, Claude drafts for review, or defer.\\r\\n\\r\\nWrite `AUDIT.md` into the vault build page folder or as a section of the build page. → **Joshua reviews the audit + gap decisions.**\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 2: Inspection report (before ANY code)\\r\\n\\r\\nA written plan, no code yet:\\r\\n- Proposed module set = Intro to LR clone, with any topic-fit deviations explicitly argued\\r\\n- Component reuse map: which main-conclusion components port as-is, which need topic variants, which are N/A\\r\\n- Content schema plan: the `content/*.source.ts` files to author and the import script (`scripts/{slug}/import-content.ts`) to generate `data/*.generated.json`\\r\\n- Persistence plan: slug prefix, records\\r\\n- Route wiring into the LSAT U app (mirror how main-conclusion mounts)\\r\\n- Build order (module sequence), risks, and what stays placeholder-free vs deferred\\r\\n→ **Joshua approves before a single file is written.**\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 3: Build, module by module\\r\\n\\r\\nScaffold the sub-app by cloning main-conclusion patterns (copy-and-adapt, do not fork the design). Then build in the approved order. Per module:\\r\\n1. Author `content/*.source.ts` from S/A-tier material (verbatim passages kept verbatim); run the import pipeline to generate data.\\r\\n2. Build the module UI from the ported components.\\r\\n3. Verify: typecheck, tests (including a `{slug}` impeccable-bans test cloned from main-conclusion), click through the surface.\\r\\n4. **Per-module sign-off from Joshua** before the next module. Commit path-scoped, push after green.\\r\\n5. Cover:\\r\\n- Topic name, slug, route target (`/bootcamp/{slug}`)\\r\\n- What content files he will feed it, and where they are\\r\\n- Topic\'s named tools, must-preserve worked examples, signature traps (his versions of FABS / Trojan Horse)\\r\\n- Which Intro to LR modules he suspects won\'t fit this topic\\r\\n- Priority order and anything explicitly out of scope\\r\\n\\r\\nCreate the vault build page, record answers. → **Joshua approves scope.**\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 1: Content intake + audit\\r\\n\\r\\nSources = BOTH: (a) every file Joshua fed it, and (b) a scan of `C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/Codex/02_PROJECTS/lsat-u/Curriculum/{Topic}/` (Notes/, Homework/, megadocs, scans) — flag anything relevant he didn\'t mention, plus a light `mdfind` sweep for topic files elsewhere on the Mac.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor every file: read it fully; summarize unique teaching content; note overlap; extract named tools, worked examples, voicy passages (mark verbatim-preserve). Tier-grade S/A/B/C/D:\\r\\n- **S** foundation, preserve verbatim where possible; **A** use heavily; **B** integrate selectively; **C** mine one block, drop rest; **D** unique-content-free, recommend drop.\\r\\n\\r\\nMap the corpus against every Intro to LR module (lessons, reference vault, indicator vault, flashcards, drills, practice drills, simulator + trap master, hard sentences, diagnostics, journal): `sufficient / marginal / insufficient` each. For each gap, ask Joshua: he authors, Codex drafts for review, or defer.\\r\\n\\r\\nWrite `AUDIT.md` into the vault build page folder or as a section of the build page. → **Joshua reviews the audit + gap decisions.**\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 2: Inspection report (before ANY code)\\r\\n\\r\\nA written plan, no code yet:\\r\\n- Proposed module set = Intro to LR clone, with any topic-fit deviations explicitly argued\\r\\n- Component reuse map: which main-conclusion components port as-is, which need topic variants, which are N/A\\r\\n- Content schema plan: the `content/*.source.ts` files to author and the import script (`scripts/{slug}/import-content.ts`) to generate `data/*.generated.json`\\r\\n- Persistence plan: slug prefix, records\\r\\n- Route wiring into the LSAT U app (mirror how main-conclusion mounts)\\r\\n- Build order (module sequence), risks, and what stays placeholder-free vs deferred\\r\\n→ **Joshua approves before a single file is written.**\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 3: Build, module by module\\r\\n\\r\\nScaffold the sub-app by cloning main-conclusion patterns (copy-and-adapt, do not fork the design). Then build in the approved order. Per module:\\r\\n1. Author `content/*.source.ts` from S/A-tier material (verbatim passages kept verbatim); run the import pipeline to generate data.\\r\\n2. Build the module UI from the ported components.\\r\\n3. Verify: typecheck, tests (including a `{slug}` impeccable-bans test cloned from main-conclusion), click through the surface.\\r\\n4. **Per-module sign-off from Joshua** before the next module. Commit path-scoped, push after green.\\r\\n5. Update the vault gate ledger + module checklist.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 4: Impeccable sweep\\r\\n\\r\\nFull polish pass across the bootcamp (dead clicks, empty states, reveal-panel teaching quality, mastery tracking wiring, copy voice). Fix everything found.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Gate 5: Parity audit — the definition of done\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `/bootcamp-parity-audit {slug}` against Intro to LR. **The build is not done until it passes.** Close every gap it finds, re-run until clean. Then: mark the vault build page complete, strike the tasks.md item, log it, and give Joshua the shipped summary (route, module list, anything deferred to v1.5).\\r\\n\\r\\n## What this skill does NOT do\\r\\n\\r\\n- Never modifies main-conclusion or other bootcamps\' files.\\r\\n- Never claims sufficiency the audit didn\'t show.\\r\\n- Never skips a gate or proceeds past one without Joshua\'s explicit approval.\\r\\n- Never invents structure or theme beyond the Intro to LR template without an approved deviation.\\r\\n"}'}]

Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\nProtocol is canonical in `~/ai-os/CLAUDE.md` (Session Start / Session End), run via two skills:\\r\\n\\r\\n- **`/pickup`** (start / \\"brief me\\"): reads `wiki/tasks.md` → `wiki/hot.md` → `wiki/dashboard.md` → `wiki/index.md` → latest `wiki/log.md` entry, then gives a 6-8 line briefing (Now / Next / open threads / suggested next) and waits.\\r\\n- **`/wrapup`** (handoff / \\"done for today\\"): proposes which tasks are done (confirm first), strikes them through in `wiki/tasks.md`, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, appends a dated `wiki/log.md` entry, updates the task anchor, gives a 4-line confirmation.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the living ledger of Now / Next / Done; finished work is struck through with a date so a new session never re-runs it.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhere LSATU truth lives in the vault (paths relative to `~/ai-os`):\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt on 2026-07-16. **Live** pages:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Teaching voice (route ALL student-facing copy through it): `wiki/teaching-voice.md`\\r\\n- The tutoring business (offers, channels, priorities): `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`\\r\\n- Hubs: `wiki/tasks.md`, `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/log.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, `wiki/index.md`\\r\\n- A student\'s whole record (transcripts, notes, homework): `students/<name>/` — see `students/README.md` for the primary/mirror rule\\r\\n- LSAT teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md` (fills by ingest)\\r\\n\\r\\n**Archived** — the pre-rebuild product knowledge is read-only reference at `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`; it is not maintained and may be stale. Re-ingest a page through `raw/` if it becomes load-bearing again rather than editing it in place:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Product context: `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md` (flagship Main Conclusion bootcamp)\\r\\n- Design system (colors, type, role tokens): `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`\\r\\n- Code/source map: `…/synthesis/lsatu-source-map.md` (regenerable from this codebase)\\r\\n- Quality bar all bootcamps finish to: `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`\\r\\n- Concepts, entities, domain index: `…/concepts/`, `…/entities/`, `…/index.md`\\r\\n\\r\\nRules: `raw/` is immutable input, never edit it. Read knowledge from `wiki/`, not `raw/`. Use the `obsidian` CLI (`search`, `backlinks`, `read`) for graph traversal (needs the Obsidian app open); flat file reads work without it. When saving deliverables to the vault, use `[[wiki-link]]` syntax and `UPPERCASE-HYPHENATED.md` naming.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Commands\\r\\n\\r\\nScripts (from `package.json`):\\r\\n\\r\\n- `npm run dev` — Vite dev server on port 8080\\r\\n- `npm run build` — production build\\r\\n- `npm run build:dev` — build with development mode flags\\r\\n- `npm run preview` — serve the built bundle\\r\\n- `npm run lint` — ESLint flat config (`eslint.config.js`)\\r\\n- `npm run test` — Vitest single run (runner wired, no specs exist yet)\\r\\n- `npm run test:watch` — Vitest watch mode\\r\\n- Single test file: `npx vitest run path/to/file.test.ts`\\r\\n\\r\\nPackage manager: **npm**. `package-lock.json` is canonical. A stray `bun.lock` / `bun.lockb` exists in the repo — ignore it, do not switch tooling.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`. Not optional before a push: `vite build` uses esbuild, which strips types without checking them, so a type error ships and auto-deploys. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on this.\\r\\n- `npm run test:e2e` / `npm run playwright` — Playwright e2e (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\nNo Prettier. Formatting is ESLint-only.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Architecture (big picture)\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Routing** — React Router v6. Routes are registered in `src/App.tsx`, pages live in `src/pages/`. `/foyer` is the landing surface. Feature-gated pages are wrapped by `ProtectedRoute`.\\r\\n- **Auth + permissions** — Supabase Auth session plus Lovable Cloud Auth JWT fallback (`@lovable.dev/cloud-auth-js`). Per-feature access flags (`has_practice_access`, `has_drill_access`, `has_chat_access`, `is_admin`, ...) are resolved in `src/hooks/useUserPermissions.ts`. Session death is detected via JWT/401.\\r\\n- **Supabase integration** — client auto-generated at `src/integrations/supabase/client.ts` (Supabase JS v2). DB types are auto-generated — do not hand-edit. Edge Functions (Deno) in `supabase/functions/`: `admin-manage-users`, `analyze-stem-response`, `generate-micro-drill`, `motivation-engine`, `parse-drill-request`, `tutor-chat`, `voice-coach-respond`. SQL migrations in `supabase/migrations/`.\\r\\n- **State** — React Context for cross-cutting concerns (`AuthContext`, `UserSettingsContext`, `QuestionBankContext`, `ThemeContext`, `TimerContext`). TanStack Query v5 for server state.\\r\\n- **Primary navigation — Foyer** — `src/pages/AcademyFoyer.tsx` composes `src/components/foyer/FoyerSidebar.tsx` (state blocks + workspace nav) with `src/components/foyer/FoyerHeroRing.tsx` (3 nodes: Smart Drill, Resume, …338 tokens truncated…ped broken for five months and silently removed the focus ring from every shared Button.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Conventions\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Git workflow:** Always commit and push directly to `main`. Never create feature branches or pull requests unless explicitly asked. After completing any code change: stage relevant files, commit with a descriptive message, and push to `main`.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session; a raw push can get rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges origin/main if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Path alias `@/` → `./src/` (set in `vite.config.ts` and `tsconfig.json`).\\r\\n- TypeScript strict mode is **off** (`strictNullChecks: false`, `noImplicitAny: false`, `noUnusedLocals: false`). Do not flip these without explicit approval — it would cascade into a very large diff.\\r\\n- Lovable-specific plumbing that must not be removed:\\r\\n  - `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin injects `data-lov-id` in dev\\r\\n  - `vite-plugin-pwa` configured with start URL `/foyer`\\r\\n  - Lovable preview Service Worker guard in `src/main.tsx`\\r\\n- Identity rules: email sign-in, profile identity uses **name** (not username), instructor is displayed as **\\"Joshua\\"**, students display their real profile names.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Cross-tool instruction hygiene\\r\\n\\r\\n`CLAUDE.md` is the fuller project source of truth. `AGENTS.md` is the Codex-facing adapter and should stay aligned without becoming a second full copy.\\r\\n\\r\\n- When updating project instructions, update both files as needed: put durable detail here, and keep `AGENTS.md` as a concise adapter.\\r\\n- When creating a Claude skill that Codex should use, also copy it to `.agents/skills/` with its `SKILL.md` and supporting files.\\r\\n- When creating a Claude agent that Codex should spawn, also create a matching `.codex/agents/<name>.toml` and place the converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- When adding shared knowledge, put it in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/` so every tool can read the same source.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\nAny change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + browser tool, e.g. Playwright or chrome devtools MCP), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Do not report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This has been the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions (repeated \\"it\'s not working\\" / \\"i dont see it\\" corrections) — treat it as a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** That means narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A responsive defect that gets pushed as \\"fixed\\" and does not hold costs a second full session — this has already happened (a pushed Home-page fix that did not survive, then 13 screenshots and 8 emulate cycles to actually close it). One matrix pass before the push is cheaper than either.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product rules (must not regress)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Foyer (`FoyerSidebar` + `FoyerHeroRing`) is the primary navigation. The Foyer additionally hosts a `GooeyDock` quick-launch row well below the orbital ring; this dock is allowed only on `/foyer`. Do not add docks elsewhere.\\r\\n- Inbox is real messaging with back-and-forth threads and PDF attachments, scoped 1:1 per student with admin.\\r\\n- Admin is fully responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Do not expose actions users cannot actually perform — no fake analytics, no dead buttons, no misleading placeholders. Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake data.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known gaps (do not invent answers)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run them with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows. Nothing runs `typecheck`, `lint`, or the tests automatically — `.claude/safe-push.sh` is the only gate, and only for typecheck.\\r\\n- `README.md` is a stub.\\r\\n- `.claude/settings.json` is set up with a permission allowlist for safe dev work (npm scripts, git reads, MCP GitHub reads) and a denylist for destructive ops. No custom `.claude/agents/`. A `SessionStart` auto-pull hook is wired in `.claude/settings.local.json` (runs `.claude/auto-pull-hook.sh`); Codex mirrors it in `.codex/hooks.json`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Skill / agent / command vault\\r\\n\\r\\nThe full skill/agent/command vault lives in the brain root at `~/ai-os/skills/`, `~/ai-os/agents/`, `~/ai-os/commands/` (split into `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees, indexed by `~/ai-os/skills/INDEX.md`). It is inert storage — nothing here auto-loads. To use a vault skill in this project, symlink it into `.claude/skills/` (Codex: `.agents/skills/`). When you create a new skill/agent/command, save it to BOTH this project\'s `.claude/<kind>/` and the vault, so the vault stays the complete catalog.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': function App() {\\r\\n  return null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * useUser — bridged for LSAT U.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * In the standalone bootcamp this returned a stub anonymous user. Inside LSAT U\\r\\n * we adapt to the real auth user surfaced by `@/contexts/AuthContext`. The bootcamp\'s\\r\\n * Persistence layer keys on user.id, so each LSAT U student gets their own bootcamp\\r\\n * progress under their Supabase UUID.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Auth gate is enforced one level up in src/pages/IntroToLrBootcamp.tsx — if\\r\\n * useAuth returns null user, that page redirects to /auth before this hook runs.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The bootcamp\'s User Zod schema stays (used by other modules); we project the\\r\\n * Supabase user into that shape.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\nimport { getPersistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory\';\\r\\nimport { TABLES, User } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\\r\\nimport { now } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/ids\';\\r\\n\\r\\n// Legacy export (kept for any local tests that still imported the constant).\\r\\n// In LSAT U the real Supabase user.id is authoritative.\\r\\nexport const STUB_USER_ID_EXPORT = \'00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useUser(): User | null {\\r\\n  const { user: authUser } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!authUser) {\\r\\n      setUser(null);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    (async () => {\\r\\n      const persist = getPersistence(authUser.id);\\r\\n      let existing = await persist.get<User>(TABLES.users, authUser.id);\\r\\n      if (!existing) {\\r\\n        existing = User.parse({\\r\\n          id: authUser.id,\\r\\n          name:\\r\\n            (authUser.user_metadata && (authUser.user_metadata.display_name as string)) ||\\r\\n            (authUser.user_metadata && (authUser.user_metadata.username as string)) ||\\r\\n            authUser.email ||\\r\\n            \'Student\',\\r\\n          started_at: now(),\\r\\n          last_seen: now(),\\r\\n          settings: {},\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        await persist.set(TABLES.users, authUser.id, existing);\\r\\n      } else {\\r\\n        existing = { ...existing, last_seen: now() };\\r\\n        await persist.set(TABLES.users, authUser.id, existing);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (!cancelled) setUser(existing);\\r\\n    })();\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [authUser]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return user;\\r\\n}\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * SupabaseSyncedPersistence\\r\\n * -------------------------\\r\\n * Wraps the local V1Persistence (LocalStorage + IndexedDB) and mirrors every\\r\\n * write to the `bootcamp_kv` table in Supabase so each user\'s bootcamp progress\\r\\n * follows their profile across devices/sessions.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Strategy:\\r\\n *   - Reads come from the local cache (fast, offline-friendly).\\r\\n *   - Writes are committed to the local cache immediately, then queued for a\\r\\n *     debounced background upsert into Supabase. Module progress instead waits\\r\\n *     for an exact account acknowledgement before updating the local cache.\\r\\n *   - On hydrate(), account rows are merged with local rows. A newer local\\r\\n *     practice session is preserved. Official LR sections wait for the\\r\\n *     runner\'s guarded account save; other sessions are queued for sync.\\r\\n *   - Blobs (audio) stay local only.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport type { Persistence, PersistenceTx } from \'./Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { V1Persistence } from \'./V1Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { LocalStoragePersistence } from \'./LocalStoragePersistence\';\\r\\nimport { IndexedDBPersistence } from \'./IndexedDBPersistence\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst FLUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 800;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Official LR sections use the runner\'s acknowledged save and PT lease gate. */\\r\\nfunction ownsAccountSave(table: string, record: unknown): boolean {\\r\\n  if (table !== \'practice_sessions\' || !record || typeof record !== \'object\') return false;\\r\\n  const slug = (record as { pool_slug?: unknown }).pool_slug;\\r\\n  return typeof slug === \'string\' &&\\r\\n    (slug.startsWith(\'drill::section::\') || slug.startsWith(\'drill::practice-test::\'));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction updatedAt(value: unknown): number {\\r\\n  if (!value || typeof value !== \'object\') return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;\\r\\n  const timestamp = (value as { updated_at?: unknown }).updated_at;\\r\\n  if (typeof timestamp !== \'string\') return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;\\r\\n  const parsed = Date.parse(timestamp);\\r\\n  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface PendingWrite {\\r\\n  table: string;\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  data: unknown | null; // null = delete\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport class SupabaseSyncedPersistence implements Persistence {\\r\\n  private local: V1Persistence;\\r\\n  private pending = new Map<string, PendingWrite>();\\r\\n  private inFlightWrites = new Map<string, PendingWrite>();\\r\\n  private flushInFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;\\r\\n  private flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;\\r\\n  private hydrated = false;\\r\\n  private readyPromise: Promise<void>;\\r\\n  private moduleWrites = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();\\r\\n\\r\\n  constructor(private userId: string) {\\r\\n    this.local = new V1Persistence(\\r\\n      new LocalStoragePersistence(userId),\\r\\n      new IndexedDBPersistence(userId),\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    // Kick off cloud hydration. The UI does not block on it, but callers that\\r\\n    // must not fabricate a default over real cloud data await ready() first.\\r\\n    // hydrate() never rejects (it catches internally), so ready() always resolves.\\r\\n    this.readyPromise = this.hydrate();\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Best-effort flush on tab close.\\r\\n    if (typeof window !== \'undefined\') {\\r\\n      window.addEventListener(\'beforeunload\', () => {\\r\\n        void this.flush();\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      window.addEventListener(\'visibilitychange\', () => {\\r\\n        if (document.visibilityState === \'hidden\') void this.flush();\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  ready(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    return this.readyPromise;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private key(table: string, id: string): string {\\r\\n    return `${table}::${id}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async hydrate(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    if (this.hydrated || !this.userId || this.userId === \'anon\') return;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n        .select(\'table_name, record_id, data\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'user_id\', this.userId);\\r\\n      if (error) {\\r\\n        console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate failed\', error.message);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      for (const row of data ?? []) {\\r\\n        try {\\r\\n          const key = this.key(row.table_name, row.record_id);\\r\\n          // A write made while the cloud request was in flight owns this row.\\r\\n          // Replacing it with the fetched snapshot would lose the latest answer.\\r\\n          if (this.pending.has(key) || this.inFlightWrites.has(key)) continue;\\r\\n          const local = await this.local.get<unknown>(row.table_name, row.record_id);\\r\\n          if (this.pending.has(key) || this.inFlightWrites.has(key)) continue;\\r\\n          if (row.table_name === \'practice_sessions\' && updatedAt(local) > updatedAt(row.data)) {\\r\\n            if (!ownsAccountSave(row.table_name, local)) {\\r\\n              this.pending.set(key, { table: row.table_name, id: row.record_id, data: local });\\r\\n              this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n            }\\r\\n            continue;\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          await this.local.set(row.table_name, row.record_id, row.data as object);\\r\\n        } catch (e) {\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate row failed\', e);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      this.hydrated = true;\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n"}'}]

= useState(false);\\r\\n  const mainRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Global keyboard shortcuts: Cmd+K / Ctrl+K (palette), Cmd+J / Ctrl+J (tutor).\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {\\r\\n      const mod = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;\\r\\n      if (mod && (e.key === \'k\' || e.key === \'K\')) {\\r\\n        e.preventDefault();\\r\\n        setPaletteOpen((p) => !p);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (mod && (e.key === \'j\' || e.key === \'J\')) {\\r\\n        e.preventDefault();\\r\\n        setTutorOpen((t) => !t);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    document.addEventListener(\'keydown\', onKey);\\r\\n    return () => document.removeEventListener(\'keydown\', onKey);\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Track scroll for top shadow/divider on main.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const node = mainRef.current;\\r\\n    if (!node) return;\\r\\n    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(node.scrollTop > 4);\\r\\n    node.addEventListener(\'scroll\', onScroll, { passive: true });\\r\\n    return () => node.removeEventListener(\'scroll\', onScroll);\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Derive surfaceId from URL for the AI Tutor surface-aware suggestions.\\r\\n  const surfaceId = deriveSurfaceId(typeof window !== \'undefined\' ? window.location.pathname : \'/\');\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { is_admin } = <a className=\\"skip-link\\" href=\\"#main\\">Skip to main content</a>\\r\\n        <main id=\\"main\\" className=\\"min-h-screen bg-transparent\\">\\r\\n          <Outlet />\\r\\n        </main>\\r\\n        <RightDrawer payload={payload} onClose={close} />\\r\\n        <CommandPalette open={paletteOpen} onClose={() => setPaletteOpen(false)} />\\r\\n        {tutorOpen ? <AiTutor surfaceId={surfaceId} onClose={() => setTutorOpen(false)} /> : null}\\r\\n      </Ctx.Provider>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <Ctx.Provider value={{ payload, open, close }}>\\r\\n      <a className=\\"skip-link\\" href=\\"#main\\">Skip to main content</a>\\r\\n      {/* Mobile back-to-mainhub pill (left rail is hidden on phone). */}\\r\\n      <Link\\r\\n [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"bfd40d","wall_time_seconds":0.3027312,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":510,"output":"import type { Persistence, PersistenceTx } from \'./Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { LocalStoragePersistence } from \'./LocalStoragePersistence\';\\r\\nimport { IndexedDBPersistence } from \'./IndexedDBPersistence\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Composite that delegates by table:\\r\\n *   - users / prefs_user / module_progress → LocalStorage (small + frequent reads)\\r\\n *   - everything else (potentially large) → IndexedDB\\r\\n *   - blobs always → IndexedDB\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Per architecture-plan.md §3.2.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst LS_TABLES = new Set([\'users\', \'prefs_user\', \'module_progress\']);\\r\\n\\r\\nexport class V1Persistence implements Persistence {\\r\\n  constructor(\\r\\n    private ls: LocalStoragePersistence = new LocalStoragePersistence(),\\r\\n    private idb: IndexedDBPersistence = new IndexedDBPersistence(),\\r\\n  ) {}\\r\\n\\r\\n  private route(table: string): Persistence {\\r\\n    return LS_TABLES.has(table) ? this.ls : this.idb;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  ready(): Promise<void> { return Promise.resolve(); }\\r\\n\\r\\n  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> { return this.route(table).get<T>(table, id); }\\r\\n  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> { return this.route(table).set(table, id, record); }\\r\\n  remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> { return this.route(table).remove(table, id); }\\r\\n  list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]> { return this.route(table).list<T>(table, filter); }\\r\\n\\r\\n  putBlob(key: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void> { return this.idb.putBlob(key, blob); }\\r\\n  getBlob(key: string): Promise<Blob | null> { return this.idb.getBlob(key); }\\r\\n  removeBlob(key: string): Promise<void> { return this.idb.removeBlob(key); }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\\r\\n    // For Lesson 1.1, transactions are scoped to a single store. Composite-store\\r\\n    // transactions (e.g., crossing module_progress + lesson_progress) currently\\r\\n    // run as best-effort sequential writes via the IDB transaction. Tighten when\\r\\n    // Module 4 needs cross-store atomicity.\\r\\n    return this.idb.transaction(fn);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b30dc7","wall_time_seconds":0.3540501,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":922,"output":"import { openDB, type IDBPDatabase } from \'idb\';\\r\\nimport type { Persistence, PersistenceTx } from \'./Persistence\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst DB_NAME = \'mc-bootcamp\';\\r\\n// v3 repairs databases that had already reached v2 before practice_sessions\\r\\n// was added. IndexedDB only runs the upgrade callback when the version changes,\\r\\n// so reopening those databases at v2 left the store missing and every resume\\r\\n// read/write threw NotFoundError. The upgrade is additive and guarded by\\r\\n// `contains`, so no existing store or record is touched.\\r\\nconst DB_VERSION = 3;\\r\\nconst BLOB_STORE = \'rr_audio\';\\r\\nconst RECORD_STORES = [\\r\\n  \'simulator_attempts\',\\r\\n  \'drill_stagegate\',\\r\\n  \'traps_tag\',\\r\\n  \'mistakes_profile\',\\r\\n  \'srs_queue\',\\r\\n  \'rr_recordings_meta\',\\r\\n  \'rr_reviews\',\\r\\n  \'journal_entries\',\\r\\n  \'calibration_attempts\',\\r\\n  \'lesson_progress\',\\r\\n  \'practice_sessions\',\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport class IndexedDBPersistence implements Persistence {\\r\\n  private dbp: Promise<IDBPDatabase> | null = null;\\r\\n  constructor(private userId: string = \'anon\') {}\\r\\n\\r\\n  ready(): Promise<void> { return Promise.resolve(); }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private db(): Promise<IDBPDatabase> {\\r\\n    if (!this.dbp) {\\r\\n      this.dbp = openDB(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION, {\\r\\n        upgrade(db) {\\r\\n          for (const store of RECORD_STORES) {\\r\\n            if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(store)) {\\r\\n              db.createObjectStore(store, { keyPath: \'id\' });\\r\\n            }\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(BLOB_STORE)) {\\r\\n            db.createObjectStore(BLOB_STORE);\\r\\n          }\\r\\n        },\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return this.dbp;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private scope(rec: { user_id?: string }): boolean {\\r\\n    return !rec.user_id || rec.user_id === this.userId;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    const rec = await db.get(table, id);\\r\\n    return (rec as T) ?? null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    await db.put(table, { ...(record as object), id } as never);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    await db.delete(table, id);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    const all = (await db.getAll(table)) as T[];\\r\\n    return all.filter((r) => {\\r\\n      if (!this.scope(r as { user_id?: string })) return false;\\r\\n      if (!filter) return true;\\r\\n      return Object.entries(filter).every(\\r\\n        ([k, v]) => (r as Record<string, unknown>)[k] === v,\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async putBlob(key: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    await db.put(BLOB_STORE, blob, key);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async getBlob(key: string): Promise<Blob | null> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    const b = await db.get(BLOB_STORE, key);\\r\\n    return (b as Blob) ?? null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async removeBlob(key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    await db.delete(BLOB_STORE, key);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\\r\\n    const db = await this.db();\\r\\n    const idbTx = db.transaction(RECORD_STORES as unknown as string[], \'readwrite\');\\r\\n    const tx: PersistenceTx = {\\r\\n      get: async <U>(t: string, i: string) => {\\r\\n        const r = await idbTx.objectStore(t as never).get(i);\\r\\n        return (r as U) ?? null;\\r\\n      },\\r\\n      set: async <U>(t: string, i: string, r: U) => {\\r\\n        await idbTx.objectStore(t as never).put({ ...(r as object), id: i } as never);\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    const result = await fn(tx);\\r\\n    await idbTx.done;\\r\\n    return result;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"65df2f","wall_time_seconds":0.2026748,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":762,"output":"import type { Persistence, PersistenceTx } from \'./Persistence\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst PREFIX = \'student\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Per architecture-plan.md §3.2 — KV via `student:{userId}:{table}:{id}` keys. */\\r\\nexport class LocalStoragePersistence implements Persistence {\\r\\n  constructor(private userId: string = \'anon\') {}\\r\\n\\r\\n  ready(): Promise<void> { return Promise.resolve(); }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private k(table: string, id: string): string {\\r\\n    return `${PREFIX}:${this.userId}:${table}:${id}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private prefix(table: string): string {\\r\\n    return `${PREFIX}:${this.userId}:${table}:`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> {\\r\\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(this.k(table, id));\\r\\n    if (raw == null) return null;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      return JSON.parse(raw) as T;\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      return null;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      localStorage.setItem(this.k(table, id), JSON.stringify(record));\\r\\n    } catch (err) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[LocalStoragePersistence] quota exceeded or write failed\', err);\\r\\n      throw err;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    localStorage.removeItem(this.k(table, id));\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]> {\\r\\n    const out: T[] = [];\\r\\n    const p = this.prefix(table);\\r\\n    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {\\r\\n      const key = localStorage.key(i);\\r\\n      if (!key || !key.startsWith(p)) continue;\\r\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);\\r\\n      if (!raw) continue;\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        const rec = JSON.parse(raw) as T;\\r\\n        if (!filter || matchesFilter(rec, filter)) out.push(rec);\\r\\n      } catch {\\r\\n        // skip malformed\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return out;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Blob storage is delegated to IndexedDBPersistence by V1Persistence; calling\\r\\n  // these on the LocalStorage adapter directly is a programmer error.\\r\\n  async putBlob(_key: string, _blob: Blob): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'LocalStoragePersistence does not support blobs — use IndexedDBPersistence\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  async getBlob(_key: string): Promise<Blob | null> {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'LocalStoragePersistence does not support blobs — use IndexedDBPersistence\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  async removeBlob(_key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'LocalStoragePersistence does not support blobs — use IndexedDBPersistence\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\\r\\n    // localStorage has no native transactions; we approximate by collecting writes\\r\\n    // and applying atomically. For v1 the simple semantics are sufficient.\\r\\n    const tx: PersistenceTx = {\\r\\n      get: <U>(t: string, i: string) => this.get<U>(t, i),\\r\\n      set: <U>(t: string, i: string, r: U) => this.set<U>(t, i, r),\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    return fn(tx);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction matchesFilter<T>(record: T, filter: Partial<T>): boolean {\\r\\n  return Object.entries(filter).every(\\r\\n    ([k, v]) => (record as Record<string, unknown>)[k] === v,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Implemented the conditional bootcamp UI in the isolated checkout.

Owned files:
- `src/pages/ConditionalBootcamp.tsx`
- `src/bootcamps/conditionals/routes.tsx`
- `styles.css`
- `progress.ts`
- `useConditionalProgress.ts`

All supporting destinations work through authored content: progressive path, lessons, guided examples, three-level practice, reference, indicators, flashcards, mixed practice, hard language, diagnostics/review, journal, and settings. Existing Activities open unchanged.

Progress uses account-scoped local drafts and one acknowledged cloud writer. CAS protects concurrent account changes; actor guards, abort signals, and stored-shape validation prevent stale-user leaks and unsupported progress. Recommendations use acknowledged lesson/attempt completion, independently of correctness. Timing is optional and derives from persisted timestamps without cloud writes every second.

Latest completed typecheck passed. Targeted ESLint had no errors; its sole unused-variable warning was subsequently removed. Browser verification remains with root, so UI is not yet certified complete. No commits, pushes, or publication.

[unit.guidedExample] : unit.drills[level];\n+  for (const question of questions) progress.attempts.push({ id: `${question.id}:attempt`, questionId: question.id, unitId: unit.id, difficulty: level, choice: 0, correct: false, hint: true, explanationBeforeCheck: false, working: 'A → B', checkedAt: '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z' });\n+}\n+\n+describe('conditional stored progress', () => {\n+  it('accepts a resumable production draft and distinguishes explanation assistance from correctness', () => {\n+    const progress = emptyProgress();\n+    const run = newRun(['question-one']);\n+    run.drafts['question-one'] = { ...emptyDraft(), working: 'A → B', hint: true, revealed: true, choice: 1 };\n+    progress.runs['practice:beginner:group-1'] = run;\n+    expect(parseConditionalProgress(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(progress)))).toEqual(progress);\n+    expect(progress.attempts).toEqual([]);\n+  });\n+  it.each([\n+    ['foreign course', (record: ConditionalProgress) => { record.id = 'main-conclusion' as never; }],\n+    ['ungraded string choice', (record: ConditionalProgress) => { const run = newRun(['Q']); run.drafts.Q = { ...emptyDraft(), choice: 'A' as never }; record.runs.run = run; }],\n+    ['impossible question index', (record: ConditionalProgress) => { record.runs.run = { ...newRun(['Q']), index: 5 }; }],\n+    ['invalid lesson timestamp', (record: ConditionalProgress) => { record.lessons.orientation = 'not a date'; }],\n+    ['missing settings', (record: ConditionalProgress) => { delete record.settings; }],\n+  ])('rejects %s without replacing the source record', (_label, change) => {\n+    const progress = emptyProgress();\n+    change(progress);\n+    const before = JSON.stringify(progress);\n+    expect(() => parseConditionalProgress(progress)).toThrow('record has been preserved');\n+    expect(JSON.stringify(progress)).toBe(before);\n+  });\n+  it('resumes stopwatch elapsed time from the saved starting timestamp without periodic persisted ticks', () => {\n+    const run = { ...newRun(['Q']), elapsed: 12, timerEnabled: true, timerStartedAt: '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z' };\n+    expect(elapsedSeconds(run, Date.parse('2026-10-01T10:00:08.000Z'))).toBe(20);\n+    expect(elapsedSeconds(run, Date.parse('2026-10-01T09:59:00.000Z'))).toBe(12);\n+    expect(run.elapsed).toBe(12);\n+  });\n+});\n+\n+describe('progressive recommended path', () => {\n+  const units = [conditionalUnits.find(unit => unit.id === 'orientation'), conditionalUnits.find(unit => unit.id === 'rules-facts'), conditionalUnits.find(unit => unit.phase === 'quantifier')];\n+  it('interleaves lesson review, guided response, and beginner practice before the next foundation lesson', () => {\n+    const progress = emptyProgress();\n+    expect(recommendNext(progress, units)).toMatchObject({ mode: 'learn', unitId: units[0].id });\n+    progress.lessons[units[0].id] = '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z';\n+    expect(recommendNext(progress, units)?.label).toContain('Guided example');\n+    recordQuestion(progress, units[0], 'beginner', true);\n+    expect(recommendNext(progress, units)).toMatchObject({ mode: 'practice', unitId: units[0].id, difficulty: 'beginner' });\n+    recordQuestion(progress, units[0], 'beginner');\n+    expect(recommendNext(progress, units)).toMatchObject({ mode: 'learn', unitId: units[1].id });\n+  });\n+  it('recommends medium then advanced conditionals before quantifiers, without requiring correct or unassisted answers', () => {\n+    const progress = emptyProgress();\n+    for (const unit of units.filter(unit => unit.phase === 'conditional')) {\n+      progress.lessons[unit.id] = '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z';\n+      recordQuestion(progress, unit, expect(recommendNext(progress, units)).toMatchObject({ mode: 'learn', unitId: units[2].id, phase: 'quantifier' });\n+    const quantifier = units[2];\n+    progress.lessons[quantifier.id] = '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z';\n+    recordQuestion(progress, quantifier, 'beginner', true);\n+    for (const level of ['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'] as const) recordQuestion(progress, quantifier, unknown; error: unknown };\n+const state = vi.hoisted(() => ({\n+  rows: new Map<string, { data: unknown; updated_at: string }>(),\n+  writes: [] as { operation: string; actor: string; filters: Map<string, unknown>; payload: Record<string, unknown> }[],\n+  failRead: false, failWrite: false,\n+  holdRead: null as null | ((actor: string) => Promise<Response> | undefined),\n+  holdWrite: null as null | ((actor: string) => Promise<Response> | undefined),\n+  sequence: 0,\n+}));\n+vi.mock('@/integrations/supabase/client', () => ({ supabase: { from: () => {\n+  let operation = 'read';\n+  let payload: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n+  const filters = new Map<string, unknown>();\n+  const builder = {\n+    select: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n+    abortSignal: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n+    eq: vi.fn((key: string, value: unknown) => { filters.set(key, value); return builder; }),\n+    update: vi.fn((value: Record<string, unknown>) => { operation = 'update'; payload = value; return builder; }),\n+    insert: vi.fn((value: Record<string, unknown>) => { operation = 'insert'; payload = value; return builder; }),\n+    maybeSingle: vi.fn(async (): Promise<Response> => {\n+      const actor = String(filters.get('user_id') ?? payload.user_id);\n+      if (operation === 'read') {\n+        const held = state.holdRead?.(actor);\n+        if (held) return held;\n+        return { data: state.rows.get(actor) ?? null, error: state.failRead ? new Error('Cannot reach account') : null };\n+      }\n+      state.writes.push({ operation, actor, filters, payload });\n+      const held = state.holdWrite?.(actor);\n+      if (held) return held;\n+      if (state.failWrite) return { data: null, error: new Error('Connection lost') };\n+      const row = state.rows.get(actor);\n+      if (operation === 'insert' && row) return { data: null, error: { code: '23505' } };\n+      if (operation === 'update' && row?.updated_at !== filters.get('updated_at')) return { data: null, error: null };\n+      const updated_at = `2026-10-01T12:00:${String(++state.sequence).padStart(2, '0')}.000Z`;\n+      state.rows.set(actor, { data: payload.data, updated_at });\n+      return { data: { record_id: 'conditionals-v1', updated_at }, error: null };\n+    }),\n+  };\n+  return builder;\n+} } }));\n+import { useConditionalProgress } from './useConditionalProgress';\n+\n+const record = (journal = ''): ConditionalProgress => ({ ...emptyProgress(), journal, updated_at: '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z' });\n+const cloud = (actor: string, journal = '') => state.rows.set(actor, { data: record(journal), updated_at: '2026-10-01T11:00:00.000Z' });\n+beforeEach(() => {\n+  localStorage.clear();\n+  state.rows.clear(); state.writes = []; state.failRead = false; state.failWrite = false; state.holdRead = null; state.holdWrite = null; state.sequence = 0;\n+});\n+afterEach(() => cleanup());\n+\n+it('blocks failed hydration instead of manufacturing empty account progress', async () => {\n+  state.failRead = true;\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => useConditionalProgress('A'));\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('error'));\n+  expect(result.current.progress).toBeNull();\n+  expect(result.current.savedProgress).toBeNull();\n+  expect(state.writes).toEqual([]);\n+});\n+\n+it('rejects malformed account records and preserves their contents', async () => {\n+  state.rows.set('A', { data: { id: 'other-course' }, updated_at: 'revision-one' });\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => useConditionalProgress('A'));\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.error).toContain('unsupported format'));\n+  expect(result.current.progress).toBeNull();\n+  expect(state.rows.get('A')?.data).toEqual({ id: 'other-course' });\n+});\n+\n+it('keeps failed changes visibly dirty and retries the same work with exact account acknowledgement', async () => {\n+  cloud('A', 'saved note');\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => ...previous, journal: 'unsaved correction' })));\n+  await act(async () => { await result.current.retry(); });\n+  expect(result.current.status).toBe('error');\n+  expect(result.current.progress?.journal).toBe('unsaved correction');\n+  expect(result.current.savedProgress?.journal).toBe('saved note');\n+  expect(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('student:A:conditional_draft_state:conditionals-v1')!).journal).toBe('unsaved correction');\n+  state.failWrite = false;\n+  await act(async () => { await result.current.retry(); });\n+  expect(result.current.status).toBe('saved');\n+  expect(result.current.savedProgress?.journal).toBe('unsaved correction');\n+  expect(state.writes.every(write => write.operation === 'update')).toBe(true);\n+});\n+\n+it('detects a concurrent cloud revision, preserves both versions, and refuses blind overwrite', async () => {\n+  cloud('A', 'original');\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => useConditionalProgress('A'));\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.progress).not.toBeNull());\n+  state.rows.set('A', { data: record('other window'), updated_at: '2026-10-01T11:30:00.000Z' });\n+  act(() => result.current.update(previous => ({ ...previous, journal: 'this window' })));\n+  await act(async () => { await result.current.retry(); });\n+ window');\n+  expect(result.current.progress?.journal).toBe('this window');\n+  expect(result.current.savedProgress?.journal).toBe('original');\n+  expect(state.writes[0].filters.get('updated_at')).toBe('2026-10-01T11:00:00.000Z');\n+});\n+\n+it('uses insert for a first account row and treats a simultaneous first save as a conflict', async () => {\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => useConditionalProgress('A'));\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.progress).not.toBeNull());\n+  cloud('A', 'another window created it');\n+  act(() => result.current.update(previous => ({ ...previous, journal: 'my new draft' })));\n+  await act(async () => { await result.current.retry(); });\n+  expect(result.current.error).toContain('Another window saved');\n+  expect(state.writes[0].operation).toBe('insert');\n+  expect((state.rows.get('A')!.data as ConditionalProgress).journal).toBe('another window created it');\n+});\n+\n+it('never exposes owner A progress in the first owner B render or after a late A hydrate', async () => {\n+  let finishA!: (response: Response) => void;\n+  state.holdRead = actor => actor === 'A' ? new Promise(resolve => { finishA = resolve; }) : undefined;\n+  cloud('B', 'B only');\n+  const observed: { owner: string; journal?: string }[] = [];\n+  const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ owner actor === 'A' ? new Promise(resolve => { finishA = resolve; }) : undefined;\n+  const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ owner }) => useConditionalProgress(owner), { initialProps: { owner: 'A' } });\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.progress?.journal).toBe('A saved'));\n+  act(() => result.current.update(previous => ({ ...previous, journal: 'A dirty' })));\n+  let saving!: Promise<void>;\n+  act(() => { saving = result.current.retry(); });\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(finishA).toBeDefined());\n+  rerender({ owner: 'B' });\n+  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.progress?.journal).toBe('B saved'));\n+  await act(async () => { finishA({ data: { record_id: 'conditionals-v1', updated_at: '2026-10-01T11:01:00.000Z' }, error: null }); await ## assistant

New conditionals are visible through existing registry-based surfaces:

- `src/pages/Bootcamps.tsx` imports `BOOTCAMPS`. Admins see all; students see conditionals after a matching assignment.
- `src/lib/bootcamps.ts` contains the new `conditionals` entry.
- `src/App.tsx:975` registers `/bootcamp/conditionals/*` behind `BootcampGate`.
- `src/components/classroom/ClassroomRouteViews.tsx:232` redirects assigned `bootcamp_module` items through `resolveItemRoute`.
- `src/lib/classroomItems.ts:194` preserves `source_id`, including specific lesson/practice queries.
- `src/lib/assignWork.ts:320` writes the resolved conditional route and `bootcamp_id: 'conditionals'`.
- Classroom Home reads assigned work rather than a hardcoded bootcamp-card catalog.

No missing hardcoded card registration found. No active Foyer implementation exists in this checkout; Classroom is the relevant student surface.

No new critical hook issue found after the acknowledged-base race fix. Conflicting versions intentionally require reconciliation instead of overwriting either version. Read-only check; no edits.
